# Repair every drill's learning procedure

User correction: the sufficient-practice route retained an old tabbed lesson panel, despite the integrated source bank. Importing 133 task instances did not import the source procedure or establish a consistent drill experience.

## Required result

- Audit every drill route. Preserve foundation 24, mixed adaptive 24, contrapositive 9, every original sufficient example and all 133 source task instances plus intentional revisits.
- Guided drills: visible rule, one operation at a time, attempted answer, specific feedback, correction before advancing. No generic ungated Next task around lesson blocks.
- Fade support: identify expressions and complete conditions; define symbols; construct the original; reverse and negate; read both forms in English.
- Independent source sets require blank production, including the learner's symbol legend. Models stay hidden until the complete attempt. Written work is self-reviewed, never falsely automatically graded.
- Preserve drafts and invalidate stale completion after revision. Saved local work is not account persistence.
- Teach qualifications, modal strength and symbol definitions before worksheet sets. Teach compound/quantified negation before the contrapositive revisits.
- Polish the actual UI at desktop and narrow widths, with readable stages, consistent hierarchy, visible progress, keyboard controls and reduced-motion support.

## Source procedure evidence

Reference notes audit: `2026-10-11-reference-notes-source-audit.md`. Group 1 zombie/real-estate and Group 2 lasting-accomplishment examples explicitly identify indicators, conditions, assign symbols, write original and contrapositive diagrams, and translate back. Raw homework and notes remain immutable.

## Current implementation scope for independent inspection

App: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu-conditional-bootcamp.
Builder: Codex with Codex workers. Existing checkout was already dirty. Base commit 67230dfae74a1466515d570fcb7b8a96ab5cb9c5 is not a claim that every difference from it belongs to this repair.

Inspect current files primarily:
- components/StagedReasoningDrill.tsx, staged-reasoning-drill.css and test.
- components/SourceWorksheetDrill.tsx, source-worksheet-drill.css and test.
- components/OpeningCourseContent.tsx, OpeningCourse.tsx, OpeningCourse.test.tsx.
- openingCoursePath.ts and content/sourceWorksheetDrills.ts as integration context.
All paths relative to src/bootcamps/conditionals.

Check correctness, stage order, production vs recognition, no answer leakage, inaccessible/unreachable stages, wrong progress, stale state, draft corruption, responsive design risks and complete bank reachability. Open new/untracked files. Do not review unrelated old changes as this repair. Do not edit files.

Host verification: targeted Vitest suites, TypeScript, production build, actual browser attempt/correction and narrow-layout checks. Reviewer has read-only source access; it cannot claim actual browser inspection unless it reads supplied screenshots, and even then should call it screenshot review.

## Review settings

Claude Code inspection requested with Opus and high effort, preserving the user's earlier model preference. At most two fresh inspection rounds. Report actual observed model separately from requested alias. No deployment, commit or push.

8	type Stage = { name: string; prompt: string; options?: string[]; correct?: number; explanation: string; model?: string };

10	type Answer = { choice: number | null; writing: string; checked: boolean; accepted: boolean };
11	const empty = (): Answer => ({ choice: null, writing: '', checked: false, accepted: false });
12	const not = (text: string, negative?: boolean) => negative ? `NOT (${text})` : text;
13	function choices(name: string, prompt: string, options: string[], correct: number, explanation: string): Stage { return { name, prompt, options, correct, explanation }; }
14	function reading(model: string, explanation: string): Stage { return { name: 'Write it in English', prompt: 'Read your arrow as an if/then sentence.', explanation, model }; }

16	export const sufficientStagedItems: Item[] = [

31	export const contrapositiveStagedItems: Item[] = referencePracticeItems.map((item, index) => {

42	export function StagedReasoningDrill({ mode }: { mode: 'sufficient' | 'contrapositive' | 'mixed' }) {
43	  if (mode === 'mixed') return <div className="sr-mixed"><OpeningMixedDrill/></div>;
44	  return <StagedRun key={mode} storageKey={`conditional-staged-v1:${mode}`} items={mode === 'sufficient' ? sufficientStagedItems : contrapositiveStagedItems}/>;
45	}

47	function restore(storageKey: string, items: Item[]) {
48	  const fallback = { index: 0, step: 0, answers: {} as Record<string, Answer> };
49	  try {
50	    const data = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(storageKey) ?? 'null');
51	    if (!data || !data.answers || typeof data.answers !== 'object') return fallback;
52	    const index = Number.isInteger(data.index) && data.index >= 0 && data.index < items.length ? data.index : 0;
53	    const step = Number.isInteger(data.step) && data.step >= 0 && data.step < items[index].stages.length ? data.step : 0;
54	    const answers: Record<string, Answer> = {};
55	    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(data.answers)) {
56	      const a = value as Answer;
57	      const [i, s] = key.split(':').map(Number);
58	      if (!items[i]?.stages[s] || !a || typeof a.writing !== 'string' || !(a.choice === null || Number.isInteger(a.choice)) || typeof a.checked !== 'boolean' || typeof a.accepted !== 'boolean') continue;
59	      const stage = items[i].stages[s];
60	      if (a.choice !== null && (!stage.options || a.choice < 0 || a.choice >= stage.options.length)) continue;
61	      const checked = data.fingerprint === JSON.stringify(items) && a.checked;
62	      answers[key] = { ...a, checked, accepted: checked && a.accepted && (stage.options ? a.choice === stage.correct : !!a.writing.trim()) };
63	    }
64	    const firstUnfinished = items[index].stages.findIndex((_, s) => !answers[`${index}:${s}`]?.accepted);
65	    return { index, step: firstUnfinished < 0 ? step : Math.min(step, firstUnfinished), answers };
66	  } catch { return fallback; }
67	}
68	
69	function StagedRun({ items, storageKey }: { items: Item[]; storageKey: string }) {
70	  const [initial] = useState(() => restore(storageKey, items));
71	  const [index, setIndex] = useState(initial.index);
72	  const [step, setStep] = useState(initial.step);
73	  const [answers, setAnswers] = useState<Record<string, Answer>>(initial.answers);
74	  const [storageFailed, setStorageFailed] = useState(false);
75	  const [finished, setFinished] = useState(false);
76	  const heading = useRef<HTMLHeadingElement>(null);
77	  const feedback = useRef<HTMLHeadingElement>(null);
78	  const item = items[index], stage = item.stages[step], key = `${index}:${step}`;
79	  const answer = answers[key] ?? empty();
80	  const objective = !!stage.options;
81	  const correct = answer.choice === stage.correct;
82	  const completed = items.filter((q, i) => q.stages.every((_, s) => answers[`${i}:${s}`]?.accepted)).length;
83	  function update(patch: Partial<Answer>) { setAnswers(old => {
84	    const next = { ...old, [key]: { ...(old[key] ?? empty()), ...patch } };
85	    if (patch.accepted === false) for (let s = step + 1; s < item.stages.length; s++) {
86	      const later = `${index}:${s}`;
87	      if (next[later]) next[later] = { ...next[later], checked: false, accepted: false };
88	    }
89	    return next;
90	  }); }
91	  useEffect(() => { try { localStorage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify({ index, step, answers, fingerprint: JSON.stringify(items) })); setStorageFailed(false); } catch { setStorageFailed(true); } }, [index, step, answers, items, storageKey]);
92	  useEffect(() => { heading.current?.focus({ preventScroll: true }); }, [step, index, finished]);
93	  useEffect(() => { if (answer.checked) feedback.current?.focus({ preventScroll: true }); }, [answer.checked]);
94	  function move(i: number) { setIndex(i); setStep(0); setFinished(false); }
95	  function next() { if (step < item.stages.length - 1) setStep(step + 1); else if (index < items.length - 1) move(index + 1); else setFinished(true); }
96	  return <section className="sr-run" aria-label="Step-by-step reasoning practice">
97	    {storageFailed && <p role="status" className="sr-storage-error">Your browser could not save this practice. Keep this page open to retain your work.</p>}
98	    <header className="sr-header"><span>YOUR PROGRESS</span><strong>{Math.round(completed / items.length * 100)}<small>%</small></strong><div role="progressbar" aria-label="Statements completed" aria-valuenow={completed} aria-valuemin={0} aria-valuemax={items.length}><i style={{ width: `${completed / items.length * 100}%` }}/></div></header>
99	    {finished ? <div className="sr-results"><Check size={32}/><h2 ref={heading} tabIndex={-1}>You worked through the set.</h2><p>{completed} of {items.length} statements completed. Your written explanations were self-reviewed, not automatically graded.</p><div className="sr-review">{items.map((q, i) => <button key={i} onClick={() => move(i)}>Statement {i + 1}<ArrowRight size={16}/></button>)}</div></div> : <>
100	    <div className="sr-scene"><aside className="sr-statement"><span>STATEMENT {index + 1} OF {items.length}</span><blockquote>{item.sentence}</blockquote><div className="sr-mini-arrow" aria-hidden="true"><span>Enough</span><b>→</b><span>Required</span></div></aside>
101	    <div className="sr-work"><ol className="sr-stages" aria-label="Steps in this statement">{item.stages.map((s, i) => <li key={i} aria-current={step === i ? 'step' : undefined} data-done={answers[`${index}:${i}`]?.accepted}><span>{answers[`${index}:${i}`]?.accepted ? <Check size={13}/> : i + 1}</span>{s.name}</li>)}</ol>
102	    <div className="sr-active" key={key}><p className="sr-step-label">STEP {step + 1} OF {item.stages.length}</p><h2 ref={heading} tabIndex={-1}>{stage.prompt}</h2>
103	    {objective ? <div className="sr-options" role="group" aria-label="Answer choices">{stage.options!.map((option, i) => <button key={i} aria-pressed={answer.choice === i} disabled={answer.checked} onClick={() => update({ choice: i, checked: false, accepted: false })}><span>{String.fromCharCode(65 + i)}</span>{option}</button>)}</div> : <label className="sr-writing">Your explanation<textarea rows={4} value={answer.writing} disabled={answer.checked} onChange={e => update({ writing: e.target.value, accepted: false })}/></label>}
104	    {!answer.checked ? <button className="sr-primary" disabled={objective ? answer.choice === null : !answer.writing.trim()} onClick={() => update({ checked: true, accepted: objective && correct })}>{objective ? 'Check this step' : 'Compare my explanation'}<ArrowRight size={17}/></button> : <div className="sr-feedback"><h3 ref={feedback} tabIndex={-1}>{objective ? correct ? 'That keeps the meaning.' : 'Revise this step.' : 'Compare the meaning.'}</h3>{stage.model && <p className="sr-model">{stage.model}</p>}<p>{stage.explanation}</p>{!objective && <button className="sr-self-check" aria-pressed={answer.accepted} onClick={() => update({ accepted: !answer.accepted })}><Check size={16}/>I compared the direction and complete conditions</button>}<button className="sr-text" onClick={() => update({ checked: false, accepted: false })}>Revise my answer</button></div>}
105	    </div></div></div>

15	const blank = (): Attempt => ({ groups: {}, fingerprint: '', words: [], left: '', right: '', contraLeft: '', contraRight: '', negateLeft: false, negateRight: false, writing: '', contraWriting: '', conditionOne: '', conditionTwo: '', symbolOne: '', symbolTwo: '', originalDraft: '', contraDraft: '', stage: 0, passed: [], revealed: false, review: null });
16	type Saved = { current: number; answers: Record<string, Attempt> };
17	const names: Record<Stage, string> = { indicators: 'Find the indicators', conditions: 'Keep the complete ideas', symbols: 'Define your symbols', diagram: 'Build the arrow', contra: 'Reverse and negate', readback: 'Read the original', 'contra-readback': 'Read the contrapositive', review: 'Bring it together' };
18	function stagesFor(kind: SourceWorksheetDrillSet['taskKind']): Stage[] {
19	  if (kind === 'indicators') return ['indicators', 'review'];
20	  if (kind === 'identify') return ['indicators', 'conditions', 'review'];
21	  if (kind === 'rephrase') return ['readback', 'indicators', 'diagram', 'review'];
22	  if (kind === 'contrapositive') return ['indicators', 'conditions', 'symbols', 'diagram', 'contra', 'readback', 'contra-readback', 'review'];
23	  return kind === 'forward' ? ['conditions', 'symbols', 'diagram', 'review'] : ['conditions', 'diagram', 'review'];
24	}
25	const normalized = (text: string) => text.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9 ]/g, '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
26	function fingerprint(item: { statement: string; sufficient: string; necessary: string; indicators: unknown; contrapositiveWords: string }) {
27	  return JSON.stringify([item.statement, item.sufficient, item.necessary, item.indicators, item.contrapositiveWords]);
28	}
29	function readSaved(key: string): Saved {
30	  try {
31	    const data = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key) ?? 'null');
32	    if (!data || !Number.isInteger(data.current) || !data.answers || typeof data.answers !== 'object') return { current: 0, answers: {} };
33	    const answers: Record<string, Attempt> = {};
34	    for (const [id, raw] of Object.entries(data.answers)) {
35	      const a = raw as Partial<Attempt>;
36	      if (!a || !Array.isArray(a.words) || !a.words.every(Number.isInteger) || !['left', 'right', 'contraLeft', 'contraRight', 'writing'].every(k => typeof a[k] === 'string')) continue;
37	      const strings = Object.fromEntries(['fingerprint', 'conditionOne', 'conditionTwo', 'symbolOne', 'symbolTwo', 'originalDraft', 'contraDraft', 'contraWriting'].map(key => [key, typeof a[key] === 'string' ? a[key] : '']));
38	      answers[id] = { ...blank(), ...a, ...strings, revealed: a.revealed === true, negateLeft: a.negateLeft === true, negateRight: a.negateRight === true, groups: a.groups && typeof a.groups === 'object' ? Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(a.groups).filter(([, value]) => value === '1' || value === '2')) : {}, stage: Number.isInteger(a.stage) ? a.stage! : 0, passed: Array.isArray(a.passed) ? a.passed.filter(s => typeof s === 'string' && s in names) : [], review: ['matched', 'revisit'].includes(a.review ?? '') ? a.review! : null };
39	    }
40	    return { current: data.current, answers };
41	  } catch { return { current: 0, answers: {} }; }
42	}

44	export function SourceWorksheetDrill({ setId, run = false, startUrl, title }: { setId: string; run?: boolean; startUrl?: string; title?: string }) {
45	  const set = getSourceWorksheetDrill(setId);
46	  if (!set) return <p>This practice set is unavailable.</p>;
47	  return <div className="opening-course-content"><WorksheetRun key={`${setId}-${run}`} set={set} title={title} run={run} startUrl={startUrl}/></div>;
48	}

50	function WorksheetRun({ set, run, startUrl, title }: { set: SourceWorksheetDrillSet; run: boolean; startUrl?: string; title?: string }) {
51	  const storageKey = `conditional-worksheet-v1:${set.id}`;
52	  const stages = stagesFor(set.taskKind);
53	  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(() => {
54	    const restored = readSaved(storageKey);
55	    for (const item of set.items) {
56	      const a = restored.answers[item.id];
57	      if (a && (a.fingerprint !== fingerprint(item) || !a.passed.length)) {
58	        // Keep earlier drafts. The new staged workflow asks the learner to check each operation again.
59	        a.revealed = false; a.review = null; a.stage = 0; a.passed = [];
60	      }
61	    }
62	    return restored;
63	  });
64	  const [started, setStarted] = useState(run);
65	  const [results, setResults] = useState(false);
66	  const [storageFailed, setStorageFailed] = useState(false);
67	  const [checked, setChecked] = useState(false);
68	  const heading = useRef<HTMLHeadingElement>(null);
69	  const feedback = useRef<HTMLHeadingElement>(null);
70	  const current = Math.max(0, Math.min(saved.current, set.items.length - 1));
71	  const item = set.items[current];
72	  const answer = saved.answers[item.id] ?? blank();
73	  const stageIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(answer.stage, stages.length - 1));
74	  const stage = stages[stageIndex];
75	  const reviewed = set.items.filter(i => saved.answers[i.id]?.review).length;
76	  const revisit = set.items.filter(i => saved.answers[i.id]?.review === 'revisit').length;
77	  const percent = Math.round(reviewed / set.items.length * 100);
78	  const tokens = item.statement.split(/\s+/);
79	  const phrases = answer.words.reduce<number[][]>((runs, word) => { const last = runs[runs.length - 1]; if (last && last[last.length - 1] === word - 1) last.push(word); else runs.push([word]); return runs; }, []);
80	  const actualIndicators = phrases.map(p => `${normalized(p.map(i => tokens[i]).join(' '))}:${answer.groups[p.join('-')]}`).sort();
81	  const expectedIndicators = item.indicators.map(i => `${normalized(i.text)}:${i.group}`).sort();
82	  const indicatorsCorrect = JSON.stringify(actualIndicators) === JSON.stringify(expectedIndicators);
83	  const placementCorrect = answer.left === 'sufficient' && answer.right === 'necessary';
84	  const contraCorrect = answer.contraLeft === 'necessary' && answer.contraRight === 'sufficient' && answer.negateLeft && answer.negateRight;
85	  const production = set.taskKind === 'forward' || set.taskKind === 'contrapositive';
86	  const deferredComparison = set.taskKind === 'contrapositive';
87	  const objective = stage === 'indicators' || !production && ['diagram', 'contra'].includes(stage);
88	  const correct = stage === 'indicators' ? indicatorsCorrect : stage === 'diagram' ? placementCorrect : stage === 'contra' ? contraCorrect : true;
89	  const ready = stage === 'indicators' ? phrases.length > 0 && phrases.every(p => !!answer.groups[p.join('-')]) : stage === 'conditions' ? !!answer.conditionOne.trim() && !!answer.conditionTwo.trim() : stage === 'symbols' ? !!answer.symbolOne.trim() && !!answer.symbolTwo.trim() && answer.symbolOne.trim().toLowerCase() !== answer.symbolTwo.trim().toLowerCase() : stage === 'diagram' ? production ? !!answer.originalDraft.trim() : !!answer.left && !!answer.right : stage === 'contra' ? production ? !!answer.contraDraft.trim() : !!answer.contraLeft && !!answer.contraRight : stage === 'readback' ? !!answer.writing.trim() : stage === 'contra-readback' ? !!answer.contraWriting.trim() : true;
90	  const choices = current % 2 ? ['necessary', 'sufficient'] : ['sufficient', 'necessary'];
91	  const condition = (value: string) => value === 'sufficient' ? item.sufficient : item.necessary;
92	
93	  useEffect(() => { try { localStorage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify(saved)); setStorageFailed(false); } catch { setStorageFailed(true); } }, [saved, storageKey]);

95	  useEffect(() => { if (checked) feedback.current?.focus({ preventScroll: true }); }, [checked]);
96	  function update(patch: Partial<Attempt>) { setSaved(s => ({ ...s, answers: { ...s.answers, [item.id]: { ...(s.answers[item.id] ?? blank()), ...patch, fingerprint: fingerprint(item) } } })); }
97	  function edit(patch: Partial<Attempt>) { setChecked(false); update({ ...patch, revealed: false, review: null, passed: answer.passed.filter(s => stages.indexOf(s) < stageIndex) }); }
98	  function go(index: number) { setSaved(s => ({ ...s, current: index })); setChecked(false); setResults(false); setStarted(true); }
99	  function advance() { update({ stage: stageIndex + 1, passed: [...new Set([...answer.passed, stage])], revealed: false }); setChecked(false); }
100	  function slot(label: string, field: 'left' | 'right' | 'contraLeft' | 'contraRight') {
101	    return <fieldset className="sw-choice-slot"><legend>{label}</legend>{choices.map(value => <button type="button" key={value} aria-pressed={answer[field] === value} onClick={() => edit({ [field]: value })}>{answer[field] === value && <Check size={17}/>}<span>{(field === 'contraLeft' && answer.negateLeft || field === 'contraRight' && answer.negateRight) ? `NOT (${condition(value)})` : condition(value)}</span></button>)}</fieldset>;
102	  }

'Continue practice' : 'Start practice'}<ArrowRight size={18}/></button>}</div></section>;

108	    <div role="progressbar" aria-label="Statements reviewed" aria-valuemin={0} aria-valuemax={set.items.length} aria-valuenow={reviewed} className="sw-progress"><span style={{ width: `${percent}%` }}/></div>

36	export function openingCourseStep(id?: string) {
37	  return OPENING_COURSE_PATH.find(step => step.id === id);
38	}

40	// Alternatives name legacy units that already teach this complete concept.
41	export function openingStepAccessUnits(step: OpeningCourseStep) {
42	  return [step.unitId, ...(step.alsoAvailableIn ?? [])];

3704	export const getSourceWorksheetDrill = (id: string): SourceWorksheetDrillSet | undefined => {
3705	  const canonical = sourceWorksheetDrills.find((set) => set.id === id);
3706	  if (canonical) return canonical;
3707	  const revisit = contrapositiveRevisits[id];
3708	  if (!revisit) return undefined;
3709	  const source = sourceWorksheetDrills.find((set) => set.id === revisit.sourceSetId);
3710	  if (!source) return undefined;
3711	  return {
3712	    ...source,
3713	    id,
3714	    title: revisit.title,
3715	    shortTitle: revisit.shortTitle,
3716	    stage: 'After contraposition',
3717	    taskKind: 'contrapositive',
3718	    instructions: 'Return to these familiar statements. Identify the indicators, write the complete conditions, and define your symbols. 18	export function OpeningCourseNavigation({ view, url }: { view: string; url: OpeningCourseUrl }) {
19	  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
20	  const focusDestination = useRef(false);
21	  function closeForNavigation() { focusDestination.current = open; setOpen(false); }
22	  const navigation = <nav aria-label="Conditional course">
23	    <p>Intro path</p><Link aria-current={view === 'lessons' || !!openingCourseStep(view) ? 'page' : undefined} to={url('lessons')} onClick={closeForNavigation}><BookOpen size={18}/>Lessons</Link>
24	    <p>Practice</p><Link aria-current={view === 'practice' ? 'page' : undefined} to={url('practice')} onClick={closeForNavigation}><Sparkles size={18}/>Practice Drills</Link>
25	    <Link aria-current={view === 'labs' ? 'page' : undefined} to={url('labs')} onClick={closeForNavigation}><Dumbbell size={18}/>Drill Lab</Link>
26	    <p>Tools</p><Link aria-current={view === 'tools' ? 'page' : undefined} to={url('tools')} onClick={closeForNavigation}><Bookmark size={18}/>Flashcards</Link>
27	  </nav>;

197	    <NoteSection title="Check a translation four ways">
198	      <p>A diagram can point the right way and still say the wrong thing. Each check below catches a different slip in the same translation process.</p>
199	      <ol>
200	        <li><strong>Target:</strong> did you find the whole idea the indicator refers to, wherever it sits?</li>
201	        <li><strong>Qualification:</strong> did your short label keep every restriction?</li>
202	        <li><strong>Direction:</strong> did a similar-looking expression trick you into the wrong side?</li>
203	        <li><strong>Imagined case:</strong> does your arrow promise something the sentence leaves open?</li>
204	      </ol>
205	    </NoteSection>
206	    <NoteSection title="Check 1: find the whole target, wherever it sits">
207	      <p>An indicator's <strong>target</strong> is the complete word or phrase it refers to. In “All guests wearing blue badges receive vouchers,” all refers to guests wearing blue badges, not guests alone.</p>
208	      <p>The target often comes after its indicator. But often isn't always. Try these sentences in order.</p>
209	      {targetExamples.map(item => <TargetReader item={item} key={item.label}/>)}
210	      <div className="cr-language-pair"><p>[Having a key] is <strong>required</strong> for borrowing a locker.</p><p>Borrowing a locker <strong>requires</strong> [having a key].</p><span>Having a key is the requirement in both sentences.</span></div>
211	      <p>The target comes before required in the first sentence and after requires in the second. Having a key still goes right. Neither the closest noun nor the next word is a reliable target rule. Ask which complete idea the sentence calls required, then give that idea its role.</p>
212	    </NoteSection>
213	    <NoteSection title="Check 2: keep every qualification">
214	      <p>Once you have the target, shorten it without losing what limits it. These two sentences test the same habit from opposite sides of the arrow.</p>
215	      <div className="co-word-comparison">
216	        <div><blockquote>Any applicant who submits late pays a fee.</blockquote><p>Applicant alone isn't the condition. The sentence is about an applicant who submits late. Drop that restriction and your diagram makes a claim about on-time applicants too.</p></div>
217	        <div><blockquote>Entry requires a current pass.</blockquote><p>A current pass is required. Has a pass drops current and would include an expired pass. A shorter label should save writing, not change the requirement.</p></div>
218	      </div>
219	      <ReferenceQuestion prompt="Any applicant who submits late pays a fee. What goes left?" options={['Applicant', 'Applicant submits late']} correct={1} feedback={['You dropped the late-submission restriction. Applicant alone would cover every applicant, including someone who submits on time. The sufficient idea is applicant submits late.', 'This keeps the complete sufficient idea. The rule covers applicants who submit late, so keep that restriction with the applicant.']}/>
220	      <ReferenceQuestion prompt="Entry requires a current pass. What goes right?" options={['Has current pass', 'Has any pass']} correct={0} feedback={['This keeps the full requirement. Entry needs a current pass; an expired pass does not meet that requirement.', 'You dropped current from the requirement. Has any pass would include an expired pass, which is not what the sentence requires.']}/>
221	    </NoteSection>
222	    <NoteSection title="Check 3: only and the only point to different targets">{onlyComparison.props.children}</NoteSection>
223	    <NoteSection title="Can, should and must keep their meaning">
224	      <blockquote>If a visitor registers, the visitor may enter.</blockquote>
225	      <p>Registration guarantees <strong>permission to enter</strong>. It does not mean the visitor actually enters. Keep “may enter” together on the right.</p>
226	      <blockquote>If a report contains an error, the editor should correct it.</blockquote>
227	      <p>The rule tells us what the editor <strong>should do</strong>. It does not establish that the correction happens. A diagram must preserve the obligation instead of turning it into a fact.</p>
228	      <p>Keep time limits too. <strong>“Before noon”</strong> is not the same as “at some point.” When two ideas form one condition, keep both: <strong>“signed and dated”</strong> is the complete requirement.</p>
229	    </NoteSection>
230	    <NoteSection title="Short symbols stand for complete conditions">
231	      <p>You choose the letters. Write what each one means so a short diagram keeps the whole rule.</p>
232	      <blockquote>All registered guests receive a wristband.</blockquote>
233	      <p><strong>R</strong> = guest is registered. <strong>W</strong> = guest receives a wristband.</p>
234	      <RuleDiagram left="R" right="W"/>
235	      <p>Read it back: <strong>If a guest is registered, the guest receives a wristband.</strong> R does not mean “guest” alone. Its definition keeps “registered.”</p>
236	    </NoteSection>
237	    <NoteSection title="Check 4: try the sentence from the inside">{insideCheck.slice(0, -1)}</NoteSection>
238	  </>;
239	}

275	function FoundationPractice() {
276	  const anchor = sections(reference(1).props.children).find(child => child.type === ReferenceQuestion);
277	  if (!anchor) throw new Error('Missing approved raffle retrieval question');
278	  const fresh = sections(Fundamentals().props.children).find(section => section.props.title === 'Enough does not mean the only way');
279	  if (!fresh) throw new Error('Missing approved fresh sufficient-condition check');
280	  const freshContent = Children.toArray(fresh.props.children);
281	  const freshCheck = freshContent[freshContent.length - 1];
282	  if (!isValidElement<DrillOneQuestion>(freshCheck)) throw new Error('Missing approved delivery question');
283	  const anchorQuestion = anchor as ReactElement<DrillOneQuestion>;
284	  const questions: DrillOneQuestion[] = [
285	    { ...anchorQuestion.props, rule: 'If you win the raffle, you get a bicycle.' },
286	    ...freshCases.flatMap(item => item.questions.map(question => ({ ...question, rule: item.rule }))),
287	    { ...freshCheck.props, rule: 'Every delivery marked collected has a pickup receipt.', prompt: 'What is sufficient here?' },
288	    { rule: 'If you win the raffle, you get a bicycle.', prompt: 'Someone claims that getting a bicycle proves you won the raffle. Which possible case fits our rule but disproves that claim?', options: ['You win and get a bicycle.', 'You do not win, but get a bicycle as a gift.', 'You win and never get a bicycle.'], correct: 1, feedback: ['This fits both the rule and the claim, so it cannot disprove the claim. We need a case where the rule holds but the claim does not.', 'The rule promises a bicycle to a winner. It does not exclude a bicycle received as a gift without winning, so this case fits the rule and disproves the claim.', 'Winning without getting a bicycle breaks the original rule. We need a case that keeps the rule true.'] },
289	  ];
290	  const conditions: Array<[string, string]> = [
291	    ['You win the raffle', 'You get a bicycle'],
292	    ['It rains on Saturday', 'The market moves into the town hall'],
293	    ['It rains on Saturday', 'The market moves into the town hall'],
294	    ['A student goes on the museum trip', 'That student has a signed permission slip'],
295	    ['The café runs out of bread', 'The café closes early'],
296	    ['A delivery is marked collected', 'That delivery has a pickup receipt'],
297	    ['You win the raffle', 'You get a bicycle'],
298	  ];
299	  const foundationPracticeQuestions = [...foundationConceptQuestions, ...questions.map((question, index) => ({ ...question, conditions: conditions[index] }))];
300	  return <DrillOneExperience key={foundationPracticeQuestions.length} questions={foundationPracticeQuestions}/>;
301	}

303	function GroupPractice({ group }: { group: 'group-1' | 'group-2' }) {
304	  const meaningCheck = groupSection(group, 'Check the meaning, not just the word');
305	  return <>
306	    {groupSection(group, 'condition-practice')}
307	    {groupSection(group, 'From sentence to diagram')}
308	    {group === 'group-1' && groupSection(group, 'Read the only as a complete expression')}
309	    {cloneElement(meaningCheck, {}, Children.toArray(meaningCheck.props.children).slice(0, -1))}
310	    <details className="co-practical-note"><summary>More sentences to recall and explain</summary>{groupSection(group, 'Say the rule without the clues')}</details>
311	  </>;
312	}
313	
314	function PermissionBoundary() {
315	  const boundary = sections(conditionalReferenceContent().props.children).find(section => section.props['data-reference-boundary'] !== undefined);
316	  if (!boundary) throw new Error('Missing approved permission and instruction boundary');
317	  return <NoteSection title="Keep permission and instructions distinct"><p>A conditional can give permission or an instruction. Preserve that strength instead of turning it into a report of what someone did.</p>{Children.toArray(boundary.props.children).slice(3)}</NoteSection>;
318	}
319	
320	function CompatibleCasesRecap() {
321	  return <NoteSection title="Three combinations fit. One breaks the rule.">
322	    <blockquote>If you win the raffle, you get a bicycle.</blockquote>
323	    <p>The rule requires a bicycle when you win. Let's compare all four possible combinations. These are possible cases, not a claim that any of them happened.</p>
324	    <table className="co-combination-table">
325	      <caption>Possible cases under the raffle rule</caption>
326	      <thead><tr><th scope="col">Win the raffle?</th><th scope="col">Get a bicycle?</th><th scope="col">Fits the rule?</th></tr></thead>
327	      <tbody>
328	        <tr><td>Yes</td><td>Yes</td><td>Yes. The required result is present.</td></tr>
329	        <tr><td>No</td><td>Yes</td><td>Yes. The rule allows a bicycle without a win.</td></tr>
330	        <tr><td>No</td><td>No</td><td>Yes. With no win, the rule settles nothing about a bicycle.</td></tr>
331	        <tr><td>Yes</td><td>No</td><td><strong>No. This is the single violation.</strong></td></tr>
332	      </tbody>
333	    </table>
334	    <p>A conditional is broken when its sufficient condition is present and its necessary condition is absent. That's why no bicycle rules out a win: a winner without a bicycle would be the forbidden combination.</p>
335	  </NoteSection>;
336	}
337	
338	function WholeConditionNegation() {
339	  return <NoteSection title="Negate the whole condition">
340	    <p>To negate a condition, describe what makes <strong>that complete claim false</strong>. Adding “not” to one convenient word can change the claim.</p>
341	    <ReferenceQuestion prompt="A form is signed and dated. What makes that complete condition false?" options={['It is missing a signature, a date, or both.', 'It has neither a signature nor a date.']} correct={0} feedback={['One missing part is enough. Not both does not require both to be missing.', 'That is one way to fail, but not the only way. A signed form with no date also fails the complete condition.']}/>
342	    <ReferenceQuestion prompt="Every covered duty is superseded. What is the negation?" options={['No covered duty is superseded.', 'At least one covered duty is not superseded.']} correct={1} feedback={['None is stronger than needed. One duty that is not superseded already makes every false.', 'Not every means at least one exception. It does not mean none.']}/>
343	    <ReferenceQuestion prompt="An evaluator with current authorization signs the form. What makes this false?" options={['One evaluator does not sign.', 'No evaluator with current authorization signs.']} correct={1} feedback={['Another authorized evaluator might sign. One person failing does not rule out a qualifying signer.', 'Keep the qualification. The negation rules out every signer who meets it, not just one evaluator.']}/>
344	    <p><strong>Numbers need the same care.</strong> If a condition means “at least three reviewers request revision,” its negation is “fewer than three do.” If it means “exactly three,” the negation is “not exactly three.” Never silently change the numerical meaning.</p>
345	    <p>When a condition is long, define a symbol for the <strong>whole condition</strong> and write NOT with that symbol. Then explain what its falsity means in ordinary words.</p>
346	  </NoteSection>;
347	}

350	function groupPracticeTasks(group: 'group-1' | 'group-2') {
351	  const content = sections(GroupPractice({ group }).props.children);
352	  const recall = content[content.length - 1];
353	  return content.slice(0, -1).map((item, index, items) => ({
354	    title: item.props.title ?? 'Read the whole rule',
355	    content: <>{item}{index === items.length - 1 && recall}</>,
356	  }));
357	}

359	function SufficientPractice() {
360	  return <PracticeTaskRunner rounds={[{ title: 'Build the sufficient side', tasks: [
361	    ...groupPracticeTasks('group-1'),
362	    { title: 'Build a case that proves the limit', content: <NoteSection title="Build a case that proves the limit"><CounterexampleLab/></NoteSection> },
363	  ] }]}/>;

366	function ContrapositivePractice() {
367	  const [current, setCurrent] = useState(0);
368	  const [checked, setChecked] = useState<boolean[]>(() => referencePracticeItems.map(() => false));
369	  return <NoteSection title="Build an original and its contrapositive">
370	    <p>For each sentence, identify the indicator and both complete conditions. Choose the original diagram, then its contrapositive. Read both back before checking. If an answer needs revision, inspect the direction, qualifications and negation, then retry.</p>
371	    <p className="co-kicker" role="status">Sentence {current + 1} of {referencePracticeItems.length}</p>
372	    {referencePracticeItems.map((item, index) => <div key={item.sentence} hidden={current !== index} data-course-practice-item={index + 1}><IntegratedItem item={item} number={index + 1} onChecked={value => setChecked(previous => previous.map((old, itemIndex) => itemIndex === index ? value : old))}/></div>)}
373	    <div className="co-choices"><button type="button" disabled={current === 0} onClick={() => setCurrent(current - 1)}>Previous sentence</button><button type="button" disabled={current === referencePracticeItems.length - 1 || !checked[current]} onClick={() => setCurrent(current + 1)}>Next sentence</button></div>
374	    <p>Moving between sentences keeps your choices and feedback.</p>
375	  </NoteSection>;
376	}

378	function Content({ id }: { id: string }) {
379	  switch (id) {
380	    case 'meaning': return <ReferenceBody number={1} omitTransition/>;
381	    case 'sufficient': return <ReferenceBody number={2} omitTransition/>;
382	    case 'necessary': return <ReferenceBody number={4} omitTransition/>;
383	    case 'must-arrow': return <MustArrowNote/>;
384	    case 'sufficient-words': return <CompleteConditionNote/>;
385	    case 'necessary-words': return <><IndicatorNote group="group-2"/><PermissionBoundary/></>;
386	    case 'whole-meaning': return <WholeMeaningNote/>;
387	    case 'missing-requirement': return <MissingRequirementNote/>;
388	    case 'contrapositive': return <><ReferenceBody number={9}/><WholeConditionNegation/><details className="co-practical-note"><summary>Practical questions about diagramming</summary><ReferenceBody number={10} omitTransition/></details><NoteSection title="Two possible rooms, one limit"><NegativeCaseLab/></NoteSection><CompatibleCasesRecap/></>;
389	    case 'foundation-practice': return <FoundationPractice/>;
390	    case 'sufficient-practice': return <StagedReasoningDrill mode="sufficient"/>;
391	    case 'mixed-practice': return <StagedReasoningDrill mode="mixed"/>;
392	    case 'contrapositive-practice': return <StagedReasoningDrill mode="contrapositive"/>;
393	    default: throw new Error(`Unknown opening course content: ${id}`);
394	  }
395	}
396	
397	/** Article body only. Course routes own titles, navigation and completion. */
398	export function OpeningCourseContent({ id }: { id: string }) {
399	  return <div className={id === 'foundation-practice' ? 'opening-course-content' : 'co-opening cr-sequence opening-course-content'}><Content key={id} id={id}/></div>;
400	}

47	type Translation = { word: string; sentence: string; target: string; other: string; left: string; right: string; why: string; readback: string };
48	type MappedTranslation = Translation & { sentenceTarget: string };

51	export function orderedConditionalIdeas(item: Pick<Translation, 'sentence' | 'target' | 'other'>): [string, string] {
52	  let hash = 2166136261;
53	  for (const character of item.sentence) hash = Math.imul(hash ^ character.charCodeAt(0), 16777619);
54	  const reverseOrder = ((hash ^ (hash >>> 16)) >>> 0) % 2 === 1;
55	  return reverseOrder ? [item.other, item.target] : [item.target, item.other];
56	}

820	        <p>Other sentences promise an outcome: “A silver seal ensures the painting has been inspected” or “A silver seal implies the painting has been inspected.” Here, the promised outcome is inspection. Ensures and implies express the arrow between the two complete ideas. Leads to, will, and always can also express a definite result in a rule, but the word alone doesn't settle the meaning. “The studio will open tomorrow” gives us no sufficient condition to put on the left.</p>
821	        <p>Try the next sentences by imagining the condition each one covers. If you know a guest is registered, what follows? If you know a report arrived before noon, what follows? Each expression will help us find that starting condition.</p>
822	      </> : <>
823	        <p>A useful way to read the whole rule is: <strong>the left condition requires the right condition</strong>. Suppose borrowing a studio camera requires a current membership. What is required for borrowing? A current membership. That's the necessary condition.</p>
824	        <div className="co-word-comparison"><div><blockquote>Borrowing a studio camera requires a current membership.</blockquote><p>Requires names what borrowing needs: a current membership. An expired membership would not meet that requirement.</p></div><div><blockquote>A current membership is required to borrow a studio camera.</blockquote><p>The same required idea now comes first. Moving it in the sentence hasn't changed what borrowing requires.</p></div></div>
825	        <Arrow left="Borrows studio camera" right="Has current membership"/>
826	        <ThinkPause prompt="Mina has a current membership. Does this rule tell us she borrowed a camera?">
827	          <p>No. Membership is something borrowing requires. Mina could have the membership and never borrow a camera. The rule tells us what we need if she borrows; having what she needs doesn't tell us she did.</p>
828	        </ThinkPause>
829	        <p>Notice the meaning shared by <strong>requires, necessitates, depends on,</strong> and <strong>relies on</strong> when they express a strict requirement. If borrowing depends on having a current membership, borrowing still requires membership. “Depends partly on” may describe an influence instead, so read the whole claim before treating it as a conditional.</p>
830	        <p>You may also hear the requirement as “In order to borrow a camera, you must have a current membership.” The goal is borrowing; the required condition is membership. “Only people with a current membership borrow a camera” and “You borrow a camera only if you have a current membership” keep membership required. Neither tells us that every member borrows one.</p>
831	        <Check prompt="Using the recording booth depends on having a reservation. What belongs on the right?" options={['Uses recording booth', 'Has a reservation']} correct={1} feedback={['Using the booth is what needs the reservation. Putting use on the right would make a reservation enough to establish that someone used the booth, which the sentence never says.', 'A reservation is required for using the booth, so it belongs on the right. Having a reservation still leaves open whether the person uses it.']}/>
832	        <p>You won't always see a familiar word. Find what the sentence says is required <strong>for the other condition</strong>. Then check that your arrow keeps that requirement on the right.</p>
833	      </>}
834	    </Section>

838	        <blockquote>A report is reviewed if it is submitted before noon.</blockquote>
839	        <p>If what? If the report is submitted before noon. Keep before noon with submitted. A report arriving at four in the afternoon does not meet the condition this sentence gives us.</p>
840	        <p>Now suppose the report does arrive before noon. We know it is reviewed. Submission before noon is enough to establish review, so that condition belongs on the left of our arrow.</p>
841	      </> : <>
842	        <blockquote>Entry requires a current pass.</blockquote>
843	        <p>What does entry require? A current pass. Current matters: an expired pass would not meet the requirement. Requires refers to that complete requirement, even if we rewrite the sentence as “A current pass is required for entry.”</p>
844	        <p>Suppose someone enters. Under this rule, that person has a current pass. That puts entry on the left and the required pass on the right. Someone with a current pass could still stay outside.</p>
845	      </>}
846	      <p>This is the <strong>What Test</strong>: ask what the expression refers to, and answer with the words that make the condition complete. All what? Registered guests. Only if what? A parent signs the consent form. Guest alone drops registration; a parent alone drops the signature.</p>
847	      <p>Try that question on each sentence below. When your answer includes the complete condition, the sentence will highlight its words. Then show the diagram and ask what you would know if its left condition were true. The right condition should be exactly what the sentence lets you conclude.</p>

854	        : "A current pass is required. Has a pass drops current and would include an expired pass. Shortening a label should save writing while preserving what the sentence requires."}</p>

856	        ? ['You dropped the late-submission restriction. Applicant alone would cover every applicant, including someone who submits on time. 874	    <Section title="From sentence to diagram">
875	      <p>You have seen the expressions with help. Now use a new sentence. Identify both complete ideas, choose the sufficient one for the left, and check whether your diagram says what the sentence says.</p>
876	      <p>If you know the meaning but hesitate over the arrow, imagine the left condition is true. Does the sentence then require the right condition? Read the whole relationship in English. If it makes a promise the original didn't make, change the direction.</p>

179	function practiceDiagrams(item: PracticeItem) {
180	  const left = `${item.leftNegated ? '~' : ''}${item.leftCode}`;
181	  const right = `${item.rightNegated ? '~' : ''}${item.rightCode}`;
182	  const notLeft = `${item.leftNegated ? '' : '~'}${item.leftCode}`;
183	  const notRight = `${item.rightNegated ? '' : '~'}${item.rightCode}`;
184	  return { original: `${left} → ${right}`, contra: `${notRight} → ${notLeft}`, reverse: `${right} → ${left}`, inverse: `${notLeft} → ${notRight}` };
185	}
186	function practiceChoices(item: PracticeItem, contrapositive: boolean) {
187	  const diagrams = practiceDiagrams(item);
188	  const correct = contrapositive ? diagrams.contra : diagrams.original;
189	  const wrong = [diagrams.reverse, diagrams.inverse];
190	  const position = contrapositive ? item.contraPosition : item.originalPosition;
191	  const options = [...wrong];
192	  options.splice(position, 0, correct);
193	  return options;
194	}
195	export function IntegratedItem({ item, number, onChecked }: { item: PracticeItem; number: number; onChecked?: (checked: boolean) => void }) {
196	  const [original, setOriginal] = useState<number | null>(null);
197	  const [contra, setContra] = useState<number | null>(null);
198	  const [checked, setChecked] = useState(false);
199	  const correct = original === item.originalPosition && contra === item.contraPosition;
200	  const labels = [{ code: item.leftCode, meaning: item.left }, { code: item.rightCode, meaning: item.right }];
201	  if (number % 2 === 0) labels.reverse();
202	  const change = (value: number, isContra: boolean) => { setChecked(false); onChecked?.(false); if (isContra) setContra(value); else setOriginal(value); };
203	  const reset = () => { setOriginal(null); setContra(null); setChecked(false); onChecked?.(false); };
204	  return <div className="cr-integrated-item co-check" data-reference-practice={number}><header><span className="cr-item-number">{String(number).padStart(2, '0')}</span><h3>{item.sentence}</h3></header><dl className="cr-practice-legend">{labels.map(label => <div key={label.code}><dt>{label.code}</dt><dd>{label.meaning}</dd></div>)}</dl>
205	    <fieldset><legend>Original diagram for item {number}</legend><div className="cr-answer-options cr-symbol-options">{practiceChoices(item, false).map((option, index) => <button key={option} type="button" aria-pressed={original === index} onClick={() => change(index, false)}>{option}</button>)}</div></fieldset>
206	    <fieldset><legend>Contrapositive for item {number}</legend><div className="cr-answer-options cr-symbol-options">{practiceChoices(item, true).map((option, index) => <button key={option} type="button" aria-pressed={contra === index} onClick={() => change(index, true)}>{option}</button>)}</div></fieldset>
207	    <div className="cr-control-row"><button type="button" className="cr-primary" disabled={original === null || contra === null} aria-label={`Check both diagrams for item ${number}`} onClick={() => { setChecked(true); onChecked?.(true); }}>Check both diagrams</button><button type="button" className="cr-reset" onClick={reset}>Try item {number} again</button></div>
208	    <div role="status">{checked && <div className={`cr-feedback ${correct ? 'cr-correct' : 'cr-revise'}`}><strong>{correct ? 'Both diagrams are correct.' : 'Review the relationship.'}</strong><p>{original === item.originalPosition ? 1	import { cleanup, fireEvent, render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
2	import { afterEach, beforeEach, expect, it } from 'vitest';
3	import { MemoryRouter } from 'react-router-dom';
4	import { StagedReasoningDrill, sufficientStagedItems, contrapositiveStagedItems } from './StagedReasoningDrill';
5	import { diagramExamples, flashcardExamples, translationExamples } from './ConditionalOpeningLesson';
6	import { referencePracticeItems } from './ConditionalReferenceSequence';
7	afterEach(cleanup);
8	beforeEach(() => localStorage.clear());
9	const mount = (mode: 'sufficient' | 'contrapositive') => render(<MemoryRouter><StagedReasoningDrill mode={mode}/></MemoryRouter>);
10	
11	it('preserves all existing sufficient examples and nine contrapositive cases', () => {
12	  const source = [...translationExamples['group-1'], ...diagramExamples['group-1'], ...flashcardExamples['group-1']];
13	  expect(sufficientStagedItems).toHaveLength(source.length + 3);
14	  source.forEach(item => expect(sufficientStagedItems.some(q => q.sentence === item.sentence)).toBe(true));
15	  expect(contrapositiveStagedItems).toHaveLength(9);
16	  referencePracticeItems.forEach((item, i) => expect(contrapositiveStagedItems[i].stages[3].model).toBe(item.contraReadback));
17	});
18	
19	it('shows one stage at a time and requires a correct checked choice', () => {
20	  mount('sufficient');
21	  expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next step' })).toBeDisabled();
22	  expect(screen.queryByRole('textbox')).toBeNull();
23	  const options = within(screen.getByRole('group', { name: 'Answer choices' })).getAllByRole('button');
24	  fireEvent.click(options[1]);
25	  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Check this step' }));
26	  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Revise this step.' })).toHaveFocus();
27	  expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next step' })).toBeDisabled();
28	  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Revise my answer' }));
29	  fireEvent.click(options[0]);
30	  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Check this step' }));
31	  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next step' }));
32	  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: /What complete condition/ })).toHaveFocus();
33	  expect(screen.getByRole('progressbar')).toHaveAttribute('aria-valuenow', '0');
34	});
35	
36	it('requires an attempted written readback and explicit self-comparison', () => {
37	  mount('contrapositive');
38	  for (const correct of [0, 1]) {
39	    fireEvent.click(within(screen.getByRole('group', { name: 'Answer choices' })).getAllByRole('button')[correct]);
40	    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Check this step' }));
41	    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next step' }));
42	  }
43	  expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Compare my explanation' })).toBeDisabled();
44	  fireEvent.change(screen.getByRole('textbox'), { target: { value: 'My reading.' } });
45	  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Compare my explanation' }));
46	  expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next step' })).toBeDisabled();
47	  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'I compared the direction and complete conditions' }));
48	  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next step' }));
49	  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Now write the contrapositive in ordinary English.' })).toBeVisible();
50	});
51	
52	it('restores drafts and invalidates later checks when an earlier step is revised', () => {
53	  const answers = { '0:0': { choice: 0, writing: '', checked: true, accepted: true }, '0:1': { choice: 1, writing: '', checked: true, accepted: true }, '0:2': { choice: null, writing: 'My saved reasoning.', checked: true, accepted: true } };
54	  localStorage.setItem('conditional-staged-v1:contrapositive', JSON.stringify({ index: 0, step: 0, answers, fingerprint: JSON.stringify(contrapositiveStagedItems) }));
55	  mount('contrapositive');
56	  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'That keeps the meaning.' })).toBeVisible();
57	  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Revise my answer' }));
58	  const stored = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('conditional-staged-v1:contrapositive')!);
59	  expect(stored.answers['0:1'].accepted).toBe(false);
60	  expect(stored.answers['0:2'].checked).toBe(false);
61	  expect(stored.answers['0:2'].writing).toBe('My saved reasoning.');
62	});

50	function WorksheetRun({ set, run, startUrl, title }: { set: SourceWorksheetDrillSet; run: boolean; startUrl?: string; title?: string }) {

77	  const revisit = set.items.filter(i => saved.answers[i.id]?.review === 'revisit').length;

98	  function edit(patch: Partial<Attempt>) { setChecked(false); update({ ...patch, revealed: false, review: null, passed: answer.passed.filter(s => stages.indexOf(s) < stageIndex) }); }

72	function CourseNavigationHarness() {
73	  const location = useLocation();
74	  const view = location.pathname.split('/').pop() || 'lessons';
75	  return <><OpeningCourseNavigation view={view} url={url}/><OpeningCourseScreen view={view} url={url}/></>;
76	}
77	
78	function DrillRouteHarness() {
79	  const location = useLocation();
80	  const view = location.pathname.split('/').pop() || 'lessons';
81	  const run = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('run') === '1';
82	  return <OpeningCourseScreen view={view} run={run} url={url}/>;
83	}
